This spec owns the items that change existing code.\n\n## Goal & Context\n\nThe model workspace (~86k lines of Rust) hand-rolls a few things that admitted or widely used crates do, and repeats several small helpers across crates. This spec replaces those with crates or one shared copy, moves hand-rolled golden comparisons onto insta, and drops the binary-snapshot layout that forced the fixture runner to decode insta's private file naming. Checker semantics, Model meaning and the semantic protocol contract are preserved; internal and wire representations may change with coordinated regeneration. Do not add legacy decoders, dual formats or old-guest support. Verify Model meaning, the current native contract, typed replay, reproducibility and safety instead. Prost-build's default WKT mappings replace in-house well-known-type generation consistently across runtime, Kettle and shared Temporal codegen: nonprimitive types use `prost-types`, Empty uses unit and scalar/string/bytes wrappers use the corresponding Rust primitives; descriptor-derived paths use the same mapping. A thin wrapper keeps the Malformed-versus-Unsupported refusal split of `any.rs`. Recursion-only matches use them: `Expr::infallible` (only its non-recursive `false` arms stay explicit), `umpire-tla` `names::rename` and `emit::prime`, the fixtures' interpreted `rewrite`. Build-time fixture discovery.\n\nuse std::{\n    fmt::Write,\n    fs, io,\n    path::{Path, PathBuf},\n};\n\n/// A synthetic control: `model.rs` holds both the Model and the golden hook.\npub const MODEL: &[&str] = &[\"model.rs\"];\n/// A shared fixture: the Model in `model.rs`, its golden hook in `tests.rs` beside it.\n#[allow(dead_code)]\npub const MODEL_AND_TESTS: &[&str] = &[\"model.rs\", \"tests.rs\"];\n\n/// A discovered fixture folder.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct Fixture {\n    /// Folder name.\n    pub name: String,\n    /// Rust module the folder's `model.rs` compiles as.\n    pub module: String,\n    /// Folder path.\n    pub path: PathBuf,\n}\n\n/// Discover every fixture directory in sorted order and validate its inputs and output folder.\npub fn discover(root: &Path, inputs: &[&str]) -> io::Result<Vec<Fixture>> {\n    let mut fixtures = Vec::new();\n    for entry in fs::read_dir(root)? {\n        let entry = entry?;\n        let path = entry.path();\n        if !entry.file_type()?.is_dir() {\n            return Err(io::Error::other(format!(\"unexpected fixture entry: {}\", path.display())));\n        }\n        for required in inputs.iter().copied().chain([\"expected\"]) {\n            let exists = if required == \"expected\" {\n                path.join(required).is_dir()\n            } else {\n                path.join(required).is_file()\n            };\n            if !exists {\n                return Err(io::Error::other(format!(\n                    \"fixture {} is missing {required}\",\n                    path.display()\n                )));\n            }\n        }\n        let name = entry\n            .file_name()\n            .into_string()\n            .map_err(|_| io::Error::other(\"invalid fixture name\"))?;\n        // Authors name a shared Model's module in its `tests.rs`;
